## Sources covered

Hit counts are what each source returned. "Candidates" are results whose name or description passed a Claude-plus-status/HUD/powerline/bar/tmux/usage text filter. Every candidate was then judged by hand against the include/exclude rule. Rows are deduplicated by source repository, so a project found in several places is one row with every place listed in column 3. Mods and plugins from multi-plugin repos are kept as separate rows.

### npm (registry.npmjs.org/-/v1/search, size=250, paged with `&from=`)

The search API stops at from=5000: past that it returns page 1 again. So "paged to the end" here means one of two things. Either results ran out, or 5250 results were read and the next page repeated earlier names.

| query | total reported | results read | candidates |
|---|---|---|---|
| claude statusline | 117,802 | 5,250 (API cap) | 1,027 |
| claude-statusline | 105,184 | 5,250 (API cap) | 860 |
| claude status line | 737,206 | 5,249 (API cap) | 424 |
| claude code statusline | 2,080,559 | 5,250 (API cap) | 1,002 |
| ccstatusline | 57 | 57 (end) | 43 |

### PyPI

- The HTML search at pypi.org/search is blocked by a JavaScript client challenge for both curl and WebFetch.
- Instead, I scanned every name in the simple index (922,184 projects, `application/vnd.pypi.simple.v1+json`) for claude/cc/anthropic plus status/line/bar/hud/powerline/tmux/usage/meter/tray/menubar.
- That gave 182 candidates. Each one's `/pypi/<name>/json` metadata was read.
- 37 rows.

- **formulae.brew.sh API:**

- statuslin.es: gallery pages `?page=1..12` (configs on pages 1–7) plus `/mods`. 50 configs and 26 mods found; all 50 configs and 10 mods kept, 60 rows.
- davila7/claude-code-templates (aitmpl.com): `cli-tool/components/settings/statusline/*.json`, 32 templates.

| statusline filename:marketplace.json | 548 |

| "status bar" filename:marketplace.json | 62 |

- 1,014 unique marketplace files read.
- 937 matching plugin entries; 776 unique by plugin and source.
- 247 excluded by hand: Copilot, Grok or omp only; game-dev HUD skills; mobile status-bar design skills; CI, deploy and PR watchers; pets; workflow tools whose status line is incidental.

**Other plugin directories the lists link to:**
- The rest (claudepluginhub.com, claudemarketplaces.com, claudecodeplugins.dev, skills.sh, findmods.dev and others) render their results client-side.
- A plain fetch of claudepluginhub's search returned no plugin entries.
- Their plugins come from GitHub marketplace.json files, which the code search above already covers.

### Multi-tool prompt engines checked for Claude Code support

| tool | result |
|---|---|
| oh-my-posh | Has a Claude segment: `src/segments/claude.go` and `website/docs/segments/agents/claude.mdx`. Added as a row. |
| starship | No Claude file in its repo tree. Starship-based Claude statuslines (for example martinemde/starship-claude) appear as their own rows. |

### Row notes

- "(macOS menu bar app)" marks menu-bar or tray usage apps.
- "theme for <tool>" marks published themes or configs for another statusline tool.
- "Published statusline config:" marks gallery and template entries.
- "setup kit" marks bundles where a statusline is one part.
- Descriptions are each project's own description: registry metadata first, then the GitHub description. Non-English descriptions were translated by hand.
